## NEW Cardinal Rule for this runtime (cardinal-rule-6 fresh-from-github + official-native-channel)

6. **Pull from newest GitHub before install via OFFICIAL NATIVE CHANNEL ONLY.** Every install command MUST first refresh upstream AND use the canonical official install primitive (no workarounds, no third-party mirrors, no shell-script wrappers around official commands):
   - `npm install -g <pkg>@latest` from `https://registry.npmjs.org` (official npm registry; NOT pinned-old-version, NOT mirror)
   - `gh release download --repo <owner>/<repo> $(gh release list --repo <owner>/<repo> --limit 1 --json tagName -q '.[0].tagName')` (official GitHub releases)
   - `git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/<owner>/<repo>.git <dest>` (fresh clone from canonical https://github.com URL) OR `git -C <dest> fetch origin && git -C <dest> reset --hard origin/main`
   - `uvx --refresh <pkg>` (uvx auto-pulls latest from PyPI official)
   - `docker pull <image>:latest` from official Docker Hub OR explicit registry
   - `/plugin marketplace add <official-marketplace-url>` then `/plugin install <pkg>` (Anthropic-canonical marketplace mechanism)
   - `cargo install <pkg>` from official crates.io
   - NEVER copy from `Z:/repos/deps/<repo>/` (those may be stale per Marker Decay corollary at `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/evidence-policy.md` Marker Decay)
   - NEVER use shell-script wrappers when official CLI primitive exists (e.g., do NOT write a `install-mcp.ps1` when `/plugin install` is the canonical Anthropic mechanism)
   
   Cite anchor: user directives 2026-05-06 "must be completly new sota and native install repos, also pull form the newest github before install" + "make sure all install with offical native sota approach, clean and offical".

   - **Pre-cite-import REVERT check**: BEFORE any sibling cite-import per Section 14/14.5 exception, run `git -C Z:/claude-sota log --all --oneline -- '<sibling-target-path>'` to detect REVERT-AND-REMOVE precedents.    - **Read-only research probe exception** (per Wave 50 fire 8 Pattern A on Agent I F-5): the sibling-bleed rule above scopes to **install-class** cite-imports only. **Read-only research probes** by sota-researcher subagent (per manifest §Section 18 research workflow) and **immutable cite-anchors** at `Z:/repos/deps/<repo>/file:line @ HEAD <SHA>` are EXEMPT — these consume sibling/upstream state as RESEARCH input, not as RUNTIME dependency, and produce no install-class artifact. Exemption scope (concrete enumeration):

     - (ii) `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/projects/Z--claude-sota/memory/*.md` decision-history files read by sota-researcher for Mia probes per manifest §18.2 (NOT installed; read-class only per cardinal-rule-10 step 2 INVOKE workflow)

     INSTALL-class cite-imports per Section 14/14.5 STILL require path-rewrite per the sibling-bleed defense above. Distinction: read-only research probe surfaces sibling state as RESEARCH evidence into orchestrator-side reasoning; install-class cite-import COPIES sibling content into runtime where any unrewritten path becomes a runtime dependency.

## NEW Cardinal Rule for this runtime (cardinal-rule-10 research-first-then-install)

10. **Research-first-then-install — every build/error/unknown encountered in this runtime triggers SOTA research BEFORE remediation.** When claude-sota-installed hits a build failure / dependency error / unknown configuration / install ambiguity / new feature requirement, the FIRST action is SOTA research (find canonical repo / pattern / cite anchor that solves the class) BEFORE attempting fix. Remediation paths in priority order:
    - **(a) Install canonical SOTA solution** — research surfaces an upstream repo that solves the class (e.g., FM-17.d watchdog stall → install `fm17d_stall_detector.py` from sibling cite-import per Section 13 — corrected from prior stale "Section 13.1" reference per Wave 50 fire 10 Pattern A on Agent I F-14 + Agent L Dimension 3 P3)
    - **(b) Cite-adapt SOTA pattern** — research surfaces a SOTA pattern that doesn't have a single install-class implementation; adapt the pattern with file:line cite anchor per cardinal-rule-8 (e.g., superpowers `subagent-driven-development` 2-stage review pattern)
    - **(c) Best SOTA-recommended approach** — research surfaces no single install-class repo or pattern; operator picks the best SOTA-recommended approach with explicit research provenance + axis-1+2+3 verdict
    - **(d) HONEST-NON-FINDING** — research returns no SOTA solution; classify as GENUINE-GAP per `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/synthesis-layer-verify.md §Reporting categories`, document in `docs/install-provenance.md`, escalate to operator decision

    **Implication for runtime architecture**: sota-researcher subagent MUST install in **Tier 1** (not Tier 5 per Agent E install order) — it's the first non-foundational install because it drives every subsequent install decision. sota-researcher is the load-bearing primitive enabling cardinal-rule-10 conformance. See manifest §Section 18 — Research Architecture for full design.

    **Build-error remediation specifics**: when codex T1 / T2 / T3 returns NEEDS-REVISION with prescribed-edits referring to upstream APIs, libraries, patterns the runtime doesn't have installed yet — DO NOT hand-code the fix. When the failure needs an independent Codex worker, first route through `/codex:rescue --background <task>` or `/codex:rescue --wait <task>` from `codex@openai-codex@1.0.4`; if the Claude Agent path hits the known FM-17.f 1M-context blocker, fall back to direct `codex exec` foreground+tee. Research the upstream first (gh search, mcp__github__search_repositories, mcp__exa__web_search_exa, mcp__deepwiki__ask_question, mcp__perplexity__perplexity_search), identify the SOTA install-or-cite source, then install per Section 18 research-then-install workflow. Per cardinal-rule-5 + 6 + 8: hand-coding a fix that an upstream SOTA repo provides is a quadruple violation.

    Cite anchor: user directive 2026-05-06 "in order to build, solve errors etc it will need to research sota solutions, repos etc first, and install with sota harness if needed. and all the tools can also be natively install if needed, or via the best sota approach you recommand".

## NEW Cardinal Rule for this runtime (cardinal-rule-12 upstream-install-priority over sibling-cite-import — Wave 50 fire 10 from user directive 2026-05-06)

12. **Upstream-install-priority over sibling-cite-import.** Every architectural primitive MUST follow source-priority order:
    1. **PRIMARY (default)**: install from upstream SOTA repo via official-native-channel per cardinal-rule-6 (npm / cargo / uvx / `/plugin install` / `gh release download` / `docker pull` / `git clone` from canonical https://github.com URL with HEAD-SHA pin)
    2. **SECONDARY (read-only research)**: cite-anchor TIER-1 SOTA pattern at `Z:/repos/deps/<repo>/<file>:<line> @ HEAD <SHA>` — immutable cite consumed as RESEARCH input per cardinal-rule-9 read-only research probe exception; NOT install-class
    3. **TERTIARY (last-resort exception)**: cite-import-AMBER from sibling claude-sota per Section 14.5 — permitted ONLY when sota-researcher subagent invocation per cardinal-rule-10 step 4 returns **HONEST-NON-FINDING evidence** demonstrating no upstream parity exists for the primitive

    **Cite-import-AMBER is NOT DEFAULT.** Manifest rows MUST satisfy the HONEST-NON-FINDING gate predicate before STAGED → CITE-IMPORT-AMBER promotion (testable):
    - (i) sota-researcher subagent dispatched with explicit upstream-parity-probe scope (probe upstream marketplaces / npm / PyPI / cargo / GitHub repos / Anthropic CC docs / ECC plugin skills / mattpocock-skills / obra-superpowers / anthropic-cookbook for primitive equivalent)
    - (ii) Probe DAG (P1 LICENSE → P2 registry-existence → P3 plugin-namespace → P4 GraphQL stars-and-bands → P5 README/frontmatter → P6 deep audit) per `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/agents/sota-researcher.md` returns NO install-class equivalent
    - (iii) HONEST-NON-FINDING return shape per `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/synthesis-layer-verify.md §Reporting categories`
    - (iv) Manifest row records HNF evidence (sota-researcher dispatch ID + probes + return) in `docs/install-provenance.md` under `effective_tier=TIER-3-LOCAL-COMPOSITION` marker per cardinal-rule-1 cite-class lattice (sibling-derived CANNOT be elevated to TIER-1 SOTA per `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/citation-discipline.md` rule #8)

    **CR-9 install-risk discipline applies at the cite-import fallback layer**: pre-cite-import REVERT check + sibling-bleed defense + version-pin (sibling commit SHA at copy time) + 2-round fix-forward budget. The fallback path is NOT free of CR-9; it inherits ALL CR-9 sub-rules.

    **Operational integration with CR-10 + CR-11**: cardinal-rule-10 step 1 ("(a) Install canonical SOTA solution") preempts step (b)/(c)/(d) under CR-12 — research-first-then-install workflow MUST exhaust upstream-install paths BEFORE classifying as cite-adapt OR best-SOTA-recommended OR HONEST-NON-FINDING. Cardinal-rule-11 META-process: every fire involving sibling-cite-import is gated by upstream-parity probe.

    ### CR-12 disposition lattice (5 classes — Wave 134 Fire 27 series codification)

    Beyond the **install-source priority order** (PRIMARY/SECONDARY/TERTIARY) above, every upstream-vs-incumbent relationship falls into ONE of 5 classes. Wave 134 Fire 27 series empirically mapped all 5:

    1. **GENUINELY-NEW** — no Anthropic/incumbent parallel exists for the primitive's scope. Disposition: **INSTALL via CR-12 PRIMARY path** (official-native-channel per CR-6). No further audit needed beyond Probe DAG.

    2. **DUPLICATE-FUNCTIONALITY** — full parallel exists in Anthropic/eee stack covering same scope and mechanism. Disposition: **REJECT-FOR-FIT** per `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/kiss-dry-yagni.md` Must-Never #4 (no duplicate functionality without clear reason).

    3. **PARTIAL-OVERLAP** — incumbent and candidate cover overlapping function via DIFFERENT MECHANISMS. Disposition: **CASE-BY-CASE**, typically CITE-PATTERN-ONLY or STUDY-PILOT-PATTERN-EXTRACT. Example: Wave 134 Fire 27-C mem0 (fact-extraction-based memory) vs eee graphiti (temporal-KG-based) vs mcp-memory (sqlite-vec-based) — same memory layer, different mechanisms. Codex T1 verdict at `.claude/state/codex_consult_w134_f27c_mem0_OUT.txt` STUDY-PILOT-PATTERN-EXTRACT conf=0.87.

    4. **PROVIDER-COMPLEMENT** — parallel API surfaces but different scopes; both can coexist. Disposition: **INSTALL as ALTERNATIVE (not PRIMARY)** — primary incumbent retains canonical position. Example: Wave 134 Fire 27-A openai-agents-python (vs Anthropic `claude-agent-sdk-python` @ HEAD `b512f256`) — Anthropic SDK = direct CC control plane (PRIMARY); OpenAI SDK = provider-agnostic orchestration (ALTERNATIVE). Codex T1 verdict at `.claude/state/codex_consult_w134_f27a_openai_agents_python_OUT.txt` STUDY-PILOT-PATTERN-EXTRACT conf=0.89.

    5. **ECOSYSTEM-IMPORT** — core primitive GENUINELY-NEW at the surface level but imports parallel ecosystem at the dependency level. Disposition: **EVALUATE ecosystem cost vs core primitive value** (often CITE-PATTERN-ONLY when ecosystem footprint disproportionate). Example: Wave 134 Fire 27-B langgraph (Pregel BSP graph execution is novel BUT requires `langchain-core>=1.4.0a2` + 6+ langgraph-* sub-package import + LangSmith proprietary recommended). Pattern B HNF disposition at `.claude/state/codex_consult_w134_f27b_langgraph_OUT.txt`.

    6. **CITE-CLASS-CANONICAL** — Anthropic-OFFICIAL educational/reference material (cookbook notebook / official docs / non-runtime / non-install artifact) ALREADY operating as TIER-1 cite reference per existing pin in `research-protocol.md` or equivalent. Distinct from PROVIDER-COMPLEMENT (parallel runtime mechanism) and ECOSYSTEM-IMPORT (core primitive new with ecosystem footprint) by requiring: (a) audited object is REFERENCE material not runtime/install artifact, (b) cite-class adoption is the CORRECT primary mode, (c) correct disposition is RATIFICATION of an existing eee rule/practice against the TIER-1-OFFICIAL primary source. Disposition: **ACCEPT-AS-CITE-REFERENCE** — do NOT install as runtime artifact; ratify existing eee discipline against TIER-1-OFFICIAL primary source; document operational-overlap for future cite-extraction priority queue. Empirical evidence: W152-F20 + F22 + F23 + F24 (n=4 same-arc 2026-05-11) — Anthropic Cookbook overall (F20 commit `4170fbc`) + CMA prompt-versioning ratified FM-21 OWNED rule (F22 commit `4ed6666`) + CMA human-in-loop ratified CR-7 + safety floor (F23 commit `04b1eb4`) + research-agent recipe ratified sota-researcher + multi-source-discovery-breadth (F24 commit `1d35a22`). **Promotion gate**: n=4 same-arc exceeds `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/codification-threshold.md:34-39,76-80` default n=2 confirmation bar (per cycle-321 expected-savings fast-path: ~3-10 min/audit × ~10-20 educational-source audits/month = ~30-200 min/month savings; LOC ≤200 fit).

    **Cite class for this 6-class lattice itself**: `constituents=[TIER-3-LOCAL-OPERATOR-DERIVED @ Wave 134 Fire 27-A + 27-B + 27-C codex T1 verdicts, TIER-3-LOCAL-OPERATOR-DERIVED @ W152-F20 + F22 + F23 + F24 same-arc n=4 evidence (commits 4170fbc + 4ed6666 + 04b1eb4 + 1d35a22), TIER-2 @ existing cardinal-rule-12 scaffolding, TIER-2 @ Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/citation-discipline.md rule #8 source-class reduction lattice precedent, TIER-1-DIRECT @ Anthropic + OpenAI + LangChain TIER-1 maintainer evidence]; effective_tier=TIER-3-LOCAL-COMPOSITION` per `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/citation-discipline.md` rule #8 MIN_PRECEDENCE.

    **Application discipline**:
    - When auditing ANY upstream candidate for eee adoption, FIRST classify per 6-class lattice (after Probe DAG passes); THEN apply class-specific disposition
    - Class assignment is determined by **mechanism comparison** vs incumbent (not surface API similarity alone)
    - **PROVIDER-COMPLEMENT** vs **DUPLICATE-FUNCTIONALITY**: distinguished by whether both incumbents cover DIFFERENT scopes (PROVIDER-COMPLEMENT — both useful) or SAME scope (DUPLICATE — pick one)
    - **PARTIAL-OVERLAP** vs **PROVIDER-COMPLEMENT**: distinguished by whether the DIFFERENT MECHANISMS suggest parallel co-existence (PROVIDER-COMPLEMENT) or competing-implementations (PARTIAL-OVERLAP requires case-by-case)
    - **ECOSYSTEM-IMPORT** is the OPERATIONAL escape valve when a candidate's core primitive is novel but ecosystem footprint creates duplicate-frameworks concern
    - **CITE-CLASS-CANONICAL** applies ONLY when the audited object itself is educational/reference material (NOT runtime/install artifact); having a cite anchor is NOT sufficient — PROVIDER-COMPLEMENT and ECOSYSTEM-IMPORT candidates can also have parallel cite anchors. The discriminator is "is the object itself an install-class artifact or reference material?"

    **Sister-rule integration** (mapping CR-12 disposition lattice → `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/codex-t1-fix-forward-pattern.md` verdict-shape lattice — table maintained in CLAUDE.md authoritatively; sister rule cross-references this mapping):
    - GENUINELY-NEW → APPROVE / STUDY-PILOT-NARROW
    - DUPLICATE-FUNCTIONALITY → REJECT-FOR-FIT
    - PARTIAL-OVERLAP → CITE-PATTERN-ONLY / STUDY-PILOT-PATTERN-EXTRACT (typical)
    - PROVIDER-COMPLEMENT → STUDY-PILOT-PATTERN-EXTRACT (typical) OR STUDY-PILOT-NARROW
    - ECOSYSTEM-IMPORT → CITE-PATTERN-ONLY (typical) OR STUDY-PILOT-NARROW-WITH-VENV-ISOLATION
    - **CITE-CLASS-CANONICAL → ACCEPT-AS-CITE-REFERENCE** (ratify existing eee rule/practice against TIER-1-OFFICIAL primary source; no install action; document for future cite-extraction priority queue)

    Cite anchor: Wave 134 Fire 27-A + 27-B + 27-C empirical mapping; codification ship W134-F27-RESEARCH-ARCH-D 2026-05-10; Pattern B HNF disposition on codification cross-model gate per `codex-t1-fix-forward-pattern.md §Pattern B` (codex T1 spent 300s budget on extensive filesystem exploration of prior Wave 134 fire deliverables; no terminal JSON verdict; ship-as-designed disposition per Pattern B + T3 post-commit verification gate).

    **Wave 50 fire 9 Agent J upstream-parity verdict (PROVISIONAL — drives fire 11 Section 14/14.5/13 row rewrites)**:
    - **Path A UPSTREAM-INSTALL-AVAILABLE (×2)**: codex-rescue subagent → `/plugin marketplace add openai/codex-plugin-cc && /plugin install codex@openai-codex@1.0.4` + safety_guard.py hook → `/plugin install everything-claude-code@2.0.0-rc.1`

### The 4-skill stack — meta-skills active in this runtime

This runtime runs with 4 meta-skills active that govern multi-skill orchestration. They auto-fire per their `description:` triggers — no manual invocation required for the meta-layer to activate. Skill discovery is Anthropic CC native mechanism (per `https://code.claude.com/docs/en/skills`) — Claude reads `description:` frontmatter of every available SKILL.md and invokes via the `Skill` tool when description matches the current task.

| # | Meta-skill | Source plugin | Role |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `using-superpowers` | superpowers@claude-plugins-official | "1% rule" — invoke any skill that has even a 1% chance of applying. Defines Skill Priority (Process skills first / Implementation skills second). Red Flags table catches rationalizations. |
| 2 | `using-agent-skills` | agent-skills@addy-agent-skills | Discovery + invocation governance for the 21 addy engineering-phase skills. Auto-fires at session start to surface relevant skill for current task. |
| 3 | `skill-comply` | everything-claude-code | Post-invocation verification that skill use was correct + complete (verifies skill checklist completion if skill body had one). |
| 4 | `skill-creator` | skill-creator@claude-plugins-official | Authoring loop: draft → eval → iterate → benchmark → optimize description for triggering accuracy. Use when creating new skills OR when an existing skill should be improved. |

### How and when to invoke skills (mandatory discipline)

**For Claude (main thread)**:
- Per `using-superpowers` 1% rule: invoke ANY relevant skill BEFORE response or action. Skill check comes BEFORE clarifying questions / file exploration / planning.
- Multiple skills: Process skills (brainstorming / debugging / verification-before-completion) determine HOW; Implementation skills (frontend-design / api-and-interface-design / TDD) guide EXECUTION. Layer them when both apply.
- Announce: "Using [skill] to [purpose]" before each invocation per `using-superpowers` skill flow digraph.
- Follow skill EXACTLY when rigid (TDD / debugging); adapt principles when flexible (patterns).

**For subagents** (per CCBP `claude-subagents.md:32-34` `skills:` preload field):
- Subagent briefs MUST include skill-awareness clause when role demands. Standing directive `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/advanced-agent-team-standing-directive.md` invariant applies — agent briefs cite TIER-1 sources at file:line + HEAD SHA, including relevant SKILL.md files for the agent's role.
- Subagent frontmatter `skills:` field can preload specific skills (CC 2.1+); `allowed-tools` controls tool surface; `context: fork` runs in isolated subagent context per CCBP 15-field spec.
- Common preload pairings:
  - `sota-researcher` agent → preload `deep-research` + `research-ops` ECC skills + `iterative-retrieval` + `using-agent-skills` (auto-fires regardless via discovery)
  - `architect` agent → preload `code-architect` + `agent-harness-construction` + `agentic-engineering` ECC skills
  - `code-reviewer` agent → preload `code-review-and-quality` (addy) + `silent-failure-hunter` (ECC) + `requesting-code-review` / `receiving-code-review` (superpowers)
  - `debugger` agent → preload `systematic-debugging` (superpowers) + `debugging-and-error-recovery` (addy) + `agent-introspection-debugging` (ECC)
  - `verifier` agent → preload `verification-before-completion` (superpowers) + `eval-harness` (ECC)

**For multi-skill orchestration** (chained or layered):
- **Chain example**: brainstorming → writing-plans → subagent-driven-development → test-driven-development → requesting-code-review → finishing-a-development-branch (per superpowers workflow grammar L7 sequence)
- **Layer example**: while implementing a feature: `feature-dev` (claude-plugins-official) PLUS `incremental-implementation` (addy) PLUS `tdd` (superpowers) PLUS `safety-guard` (ECC) PLUS `verification-before-completion` (superpowers)
- **Anti-pattern**: invoking ONLY one skill when 2-4 apply. The 1% rule is bidirectional — even if uncertain whether skill X applies, invoke it; if it turns out wrong, drop it.

### Default skill catalog by domain (1556 SKILL.md across 21 active plugins + 14 marketplaces)

When uncertain which skill applies, the meta-skill `using-agent-skills` auto-fires and surfaces relevant skills via description matching. Operator can also explicitly query via `Skill <name>` slash command.

| Domain | Primary skills (auto-fire candidates) |

| Engineering phases | addy-agent-skills: api-and-interface-design / browser-testing-with-devtools / ci-cd-and-automation / code-review-and-quality / context-engineering / debugging-and-error-recovery / deprecation-and-migration / documentation-and-adrs / frontend-ui-engineering / git-workflow-and-versioning / idea-refine / incremental-implementation / performance-optimization / planning-and-task-breakdown / security-and-hardening / shipping-and-launch / source-driven-development / spec-driven-development / test-driven-development / using-agent-skills |
| ECC autonomous-loop | everything-claude-code: agentic-engineering / autonomous-loops / autonomous-agent-harness / continuous-learning / continuous-agent-loop / gateguard / safety-guard / agent-eval / agent-introspection-debugging / coding-standards / research-ops / deep-research / ai-first-engineering / exa-search / iterative-retrieval / council |

### Update triggers

Re-evaluate this skill orchestration discipline when:
- A 5th meta-skill emerges in a registered marketplace (e.g., `using-mcp-skills` or `using-claude-md-skills`) — add to the 4-skill stack
- Anthropic CC ships a `skills` runtime probe primitive (e.g., a `claude skills list` CLI subcommand or SessionStart auto-injection) that obviates manual `using-agent-skills` auto-fire
- A subagent class consistently fails to invoke a relevant skill — promote that skill into the agent's frontmatter `skills:` preload
- A documented skill conflict surfaces between auto-firing meta-skills (e.g., `using-superpowers` + `using-agent-skills` both demand precedence) — codify priority order in superpowers/using-superpowers Red Flags table
- A new SOTA repo ships a competing skill-orchestration discipline (e.g., openai/codex-skills) — re-audit Axis 1 multi-org convergence per `convergence-gate.md`

# AGENTS.md — claude-sota-installed (eee runtime)

> Cross-agent contract for the eee SOTA Claude Code runtime. Per the
> [agents.md/](https://agents.md) standard (60k+ open-source projects). This file
> is the canonical surface for **Codex CLI / Cursor / Gemini CLI / Antigravity /
> Aider / Kilo Code / Cline / GitHub Copilot CLI** consuming this repo. Claude Code
> reads `CLAUDE.md` (cardinal rules) which is authoritative for Claude-Code-only
> discipline; this file is the cross-tool intersection.
>
> **Closest-AGENTS.md-wins precedence** per agents.md/ spec — sub-trees may override.

## Project overview

`claude-sota-installed` is a Z:-portable, install-only canonical SOTA Claude Code
runtime. **Bootstrap-only files are hand-coded** (CLAUDE.md / CLAUDE.local.md /
tools/eee.ps1 / bin/eee.cmd / .claude/settings.json minimum baseline / .gitignore +
the docs/ install-discipline files); **all other primitives MUST come from upstream
SOTA installs** per cardinal-rule-5 (plugins, MCPs, hooks, skills, agents).

## Testing instructions

- **Cross-model gate (cardinal-rule-3)**: every architectural commit on a design
  surface MUST pass codex T1 pre-edit consult OR T2 commit-time hook gate.
  Foreground+tee path: `codex exec --ephemeral -p deep-review-exec --color never <
  .claude/state/codex_consult_<topic>.txt > .claude/state/codex_consult_<topic>_OUT.txt 2>&1`
- **Pattern A fix-forward** (per `Z:\claude-sota\.claude\rules\codex-t1-fix-forward-pattern.md`):
  on NEEDS-REVISION conf 0.88-0.93 with ≤10 prescriptions, apply ALL in single
  atomic commit (no iter-N→N.1 loops)
- **Pattern B HONEST-NON-FINDING**: T1 timeout-without-JSON-verdict → trace-mine for
  embedded evidence + ship as-designed + T2/T3 verify on commit/post-commit
- **Mia pre-apply** (per `mia-pre-apply.md`): every prescription with `prescribed_edits`
  / specific file:line claims gets cheap-probe verification BEFORE Edit (n=48
  cumulative OVER catches Wave 97-118)
- **No new tests required for descriptive doc edits** — runtime probe (`Read +
  Grep`) is verification

## Code style guidelines

- **No comments unless WHY is non-obvious** (CLAUDE.md hard rule)
- **No speculative helpers / abstractions** (kiss-dry-yagni)
- **Three similar lines is better than premature abstraction** — defer DRY until
  ≥3 actual call sites
- **Cite SOTA primary at file:line + HEAD SHA** for every architectural claim
  (cardinal-rule-1)
- **Evidence markers** required on volatile claims: `[VERIFIED]` / `[INFERRED]` /
  `[UNKNOWN]` / `[MEASURED]` / `[REFUTED]` per `evidence-policy.md`

## Security considerations (cardinal hard-rules)

- **NEVER commit secrets** — `.cli-proxy-api/` / `*.env` / `*.pem` / `*.key` /
  `secrets/**` are gitignored and `.claude/settings.json:permissions.deny[]` blocks
  reads
- **`safety_guard.py`** PreToolUse:Bash hook blocks 12 destructive patterns
  (`rm -rf` / `git push --force` / `git reset --hard` / SQL DROP / `chmod 777` /
  `mkfs` / `dd` / fork bomb / etc.)
- **`agent_plan_readonly_bash_guard.py`** restricts subagent Bash to readonly when
  `permissionMode: plan` — operator path retains full Bash via main session
- **`gitleaks_pre_commit_gate.py`** blocks `git commit`/`git push` if secrets
  detected
- **`codex_t2_pre_commit_gate.py`** cross-model gate on every commit
- **NEVER use `--no-verify`** to bypass hooks (canonical Must-Never #3); fix the
  hook OR the underlying issue

## PR / commit instructions

- **Conventional commits**: `feat(scope)` / `fix(scope)` / `docs(scope)` / `chore` /
  `refactor` / `test` / `perf`
- **Wave-N attribution**: include `Wave <N> Ship <ID>` in subject; commit body
  includes CR-1..CR-12 conformance + Mia OVER catches + cross-model T1 verdict
  pointer
- **Atomic single-shell commits** per `audit-action-loop.md` step 4 + `git-cli-grammar-discipline.md`:
  `git add -- <file> && git commit -o -F msg.txt -- <file>`
- **One logical unit per commit** per cycle-300 ONE-LOGICAL-UNIT-PER-FIRE
- **No PR title format constraint** — solo eee runtime

## Done criteria (cross-agent handoff contract)

A change ships clean when ALL hold:

- ✅ Diff is minimal and scoped to the user's request
- ✅ Quality gates pass OR failures are documented in commit body
- ✅ Codex review findings are triaged (APPROVE / NEEDS-REVISION applied / Pattern B
  HNF documented)
- ✅ No new secrets, unsafe permissions, unbounded MCPs, or hidden memory writes
- ✅ Handoff includes: goal, files touched, commands run, test results, unresolved
  risks, next actions
- ✅ Mia pre-apply probe passed on every prescription before Edit
- ✅ TIER-1-DIRECT cite trail per cardinal-rule-1

37:8. **Composed-claims discipline + mechanical source-class reduction lattice** — *(Wave 17 fire-31 v1 codification per codex T1 fire-28 conf=0.87; UPGRADED at fire-36 per codex T1 fire-33 NEEDS-REVISION on T2 b8fa7dd1 finding "weakest-source rule not computable" conf=0.72)* — When a single normative claim cites MULTIPLE source classes, the cite MUST declare:
38:   - `constituents=[<class1@cite1>, <class2@cite2>, ...]` — explicit list of each constituent primitive at file:line + SHA OR official-docs URL
39:   - `effective_tier=<class>` — the EFFECTIVE class of the composed claim, computed by the reduction lattice below
43:   2. `TIER-3-LOCAL-COMPOSITION` (any local glue / sss-novel composition over TIER-1/TIER-2 substrates)
47:   6. `TIER-1-COMPOSED` (ALL constituents TIER-1 with disclosed local glue) — still TIER-3-LOCAL-COMPOSITION as effective tier
50:   **Reduction rule (mechanical)**: `effective_tier = MIN_PRECEDENCE(constituents)`. Concretely: if ANY constituent contains "TIER-3-LOCAL-*" OR the composition glue itself is local-codified (e.g., sss-novel composition pattern), the effective tier is `TIER-3-LOCAL-COMPOSITION` regardless of how many TIER-1 primitives are cited. EXCEPTION: if the composed claim is ONLY about an upstream primitive's EXISTENCE (e.g., "asyncRewake field exists" cited verbatim from Anthropic docs), it remains `TIER-1-DIRECT` because there's no local composition glue.
52:   **Anti-promotion rule**: composition CANNOT promote a TIER-2/TIER-3 component into TIER-1. The cite class of the COMPOSED claim is determined by the MIN_PRECEDENCE constituent OR by the composition-glue class, whichever is weaker.
55:   - `constituents=[TIER-1-DIRECT @ Anthropic-CC-hooks-docs-L1021-1023, TIER-2 @ CCBP-cross-model-workflow.md:1-48, TIER-3-LOCAL-COMPOSITION @ sss-novel T0+T7+profile-selection]`
56:   - `effective_tier=TIER-3-LOCAL-COMPOSITION` (MIN_PRECEDENCE = 2 due to sss-novel glue)
57:   - Despite Anthropic CC + CCBP being upstream-authority for primitives, the composition itself is sss-local and EFFECTIVE class is TIER-3-LOCAL-COMPOSITION

66:## Subclaim-type discriminator (UPSTREAM / OPERATIONAL / CATEGORY)
72:| **UPSTREAM-CLAIM** | About external project's state (README content, repo metadata, framing) — subject to upstream evolution between verdict-cycle and probe-cycle | HIGH refutation candidate — upstream may have reformed |
73:| **OPERATIONAL-CLAIM** | About OUR harness state (live MCP invocations, hook-fire counts, integration depth) — controlled by us, slow-moving | LOW refutation likelihood — state changes only when we change it |
74:| **CATEGORY-CLAIM** | About semantic mismatch between candidate and our use-case (RAG vs session-memory, batch vs streaming) — requires upstream pivot to flip | LOW refutation likelihood — categorical signals stable |
76:**Mixed types**: a single candidate's subclaim chain can include all three. CATEGORY-CLAIM ("API-redundant with X") + OPERATIONAL-CLAIM ("our X integration is live") can both apply to the same exclusion verdict.
79:1. Type the claim — UPSTREAM / OPERATIONAL / CATEGORY?
80:2. Estimate probe budget — UPSTREAM gets full 5-axis (WebFetch README + GitHub API + convergence-gate axes); OPERATIONAL gets spot-check via local grep; CATEGORY gets one-pass README scan
81:3. Honest classification — if uncertain, default to UPSTREAM-CLAIM (under-probing has higher false-PASS cost than over-probing)
83:**Distinguishes from §Reporting categories above**: §Reporting categories classifies REPORT shape (OVER/UNDER/HNF — about agent's output). §Subclaim-type discriminator classifies SOURCE shape (UPSTREAM/OPERATIONAL/CATEGORY — about what's being verified). Orthogonal axes — a UPSTREAM-CLAIM probe may produce HNF, OVER, or UNDER report.
87:After typing the subclaim by source as UPSTREAM / OPERATIONAL / CATEGORY, add **SHAPE-CLAIM** when the claim depends on **structured output that can be checked before deployment**: TypeScript workflow code, Pydantic / BaseModel payloads, Zod / Effect schemas, JSON Schema validated tool args, `PINS.json` entries, `settings.json` hook entries, `.mcp.json` server configs, MCP tool args, or hook JSONL payloads.
90:- `UPSTREAM-CLAIM + SHAPE-CLAIM`: PINS.json bumps citing upstream HEAD SHA + content-SHA (the bump is upstream-state; the content shape is checkable)
91:- `OPERATIONAL-CLAIM + SHAPE-CLAIM`: settings.json hook entries, .mcp.json server configs, hook JSONL payloads (the hook fires per harness state; the JSON shape validates)
92:- `CATEGORY-CLAIM + SHAPE-CLAIM`: agent frontmatter or skill SKILL.md (the frontmatter is structured + parseable; the categorical role is semantic)

# SOTA-installed manifest — single source of truth for claude-sota-installed

> **Purpose**: every architectural primitive in `claude-sota-installed` is tracked here with: install or cite-import command + upstream HEAD SHA + axis 1+2+3 verdict + Probe DAG outcome + smoke-probe verification + disposition. Per cardinal-rule-5 install-priority + cardinal-rule-6 fresh-from-github + cardinal-rule "zero self-inventing" (user directive 2026-05-06).
>
> **Two install classes** (per user directive 2026-05-06 "all the sources form advanced repos, and guide"):
>
> - **INSTALL-CLASS** — code/binary that RUNS in the runtime (plugin / MCP server / CLI binary / direct .claude/* import / hook script). Lives under `.claude/plugins/cache/` / `.mcp.json` / system PATH / `.claude/{rules,agents,skills,commands,hooks/scripts}/`.
> - **CITE-CLASS** — documentation/guide that authorities a primitive without running. Cited at `<repo>/<file>:<line> @ HEAD <SHA>` in this manifest's "Source guide" column. Examples: CCBP `claude-settings.md`, Karpathy gist, Boris Cherny tips.
>
> Every row carries BOTH classes where applicable (the install + the guide it implements).

## Status legend

- `INSTALLED` — install complete + smoke-probe PASS + axis-1+2+3 verified
- `INSTALLED-VIA-SYSTEM-PATH` — primitive available via system PATH (winget / chocolatey / package-manager-system-scope) at acceptable freshness per cardinal-rule-6; version anchor + smoke probe + axis verdict captured in install-provenance.md (added Wave 50 fire 4 per Agent C-redo P1-3 — fire 3 PATH-discovery introduced this disposition)
- `INSTALLED-AMBER` — install complete but Probe DAG flagged P2/P3 issue; manifest carries the deferred fix
- `CITE-IMPORT-AMBER` — sibling cite-import per Section 14 / 14.5 exception; subagent or hook copied with sibling commit-SHA cite trail; re-evaluate when sibling commit-SHA churns >50% post-import
- `STAGED` — install command run but smoke-probe pending
- `DEFERRED-PENDING-FIX` — install blocked on upstream issue (e.g., breaking change, license incompat); cite-import permitted as substitute pending fix
- `REJECTED-POST-PROBE` — Probe DAG returned REJECT-FOR-FIT or axis-fail; cite-only retained for forward research
- `PLANNED` — design phase; install command drafted but not yet run
- `CITE-ONLY` — guide source cited as authority; no install needed (e.g., CCBP cardinal-rule cites)

### Status legend extension — Wave 146 Fire 3 refinement (W146-F3 method-extension, NOT Agent A verbatim)

**Per W146-F3 codex T1 NEEDS-REVISION conf=0.91 MODIFY #1** [VERIFIED via `.claude/state/codex_consult_w146_f3_p1_manifest_hygiene_OUT.txt` JSON EOF]: this section EXTENDS the legend above with 2 NEW distinguishing classes (`INSTALLED-ACTIVE` / `INSTALLED-DORMANT`) per Agent C UNDER-claim #2 from Wave 146 Fire 1, AND codifies reproducible SOTA-cleanliness scoring weights. ### SOTA-cleanliness scoring (W146-F3 method-refinement of W146-F1 Agent A baseline 61%)

Per W146-F1 Agent A baseline (61% overall / band 55-65% per Agent C calibration) + W146-F3 method-refinement: future cleanliness % audits MUST disclose (a) total rows counted, (b) rows excluded (HISTORICAL-MACHINE-EXCLUDED markers + REJECTED-POST-PROBE), (c) per-class counts, (d) computed weighted %, (e) cite-class chain. **Reproducibility predicate**: same input manifest + same method → same output %. **Method-change disclosure**: Agent A treated AMBER/PARTIAL/DEFERRED/REJECTED as 0 clean (verbatim); W146-F3 introduces fractional +0.5 weights for INSTALLED-DORMANT/INSTALLED-AMBER/INSTALLED-PARTIAL — disclose as W146-F3 refinement when re-computing % under this method, NOT Agent A verbatim.

## Mechanical-mirror exception — T1 SKIP for downstream-propagation ships (n=2 self-observed + n=1 user-triggered codification — same-session 2026-05-02)

T1 may be skipped only when the edit is pure downstream-propagation of a **settled upstream authority** to a sister/parallel surface (rule-layer ↔ skill-layer ↔ canonical-authority-surface). **Settled** means either (a) the upstream concept has terminal T1 APPROVE, or NEEDS-REVISION with all prescribed edits integrated, cited with `[VERIFIED via <verdict path>]` plus commit SHA, or (b) an explicit user-trigger directive is being copied as recorded authority with no interpretation. REJECT, INCONCLUSIVE, UNKNOWN, timeout-with-red-flags, or NEEDS-REVISION-with-open-prescriptions fail closed to fresh T1. This is a scoped mechanical-mirror exception, not a generic "looks mechanical" exception. T2 commit-time hook (`.claude/hooks/scripts/codex_t2_pre_commit_gate.py:424` PreToolUse `Bash(git commit *)` per `.claude/rules/layered-gates-architecture.md` §5 Layer 2) is the cross-model verification net.

### Activation predicates (ALL required)

1. **Single-file ship + ≤24 LOC delta** — bounded scope per `.claude/rules/codification-threshold.md` cycle-322 jurisdiction (200-LOC ceiling honored)
2. **Pure pointer-extension** — NO new activation criteria, thresholds, lifecycle semantics, enforcement behavior, source-admissibility rules, recovery mechanics, or verdict-disposition rules inline. The new content is limited to naming the existing authority, adding a pointer, and stating: "Mechanics live in the cited canonical authority surfaces; DO NOT duplicate them here per `.claude/rules/kiss-dry-yagni.md` §Don't Repeat Yourself + `.claude/rules/named-failure-modes.md` catalog/router discipline."
3. **NO new authority/cite/status class introduced** — every new pointer has 1:1 cite to an already-settled rule/skill/command/script or explicit user-trigger authority; no fresh upstream source, new TIER-1/TIER-2/TIER-3 cite class, new hook-status claim, new verdict-confidence claim, new source-admissibility rule, or new canonical-owner claim is introduced.
4. **Mirror chain context** — edit is part of a deliberate session-arc mirror cycle (n≥2 prior mirror ships within session) OR an explicit user-trigger directive points to an already-recorded authority/memory to mirror. User-trigger supplies trigger legitimacy only; it does not waive predicates #1-#3 or the settled-source requirement.
5. **Target-surface compatibility preflight** — before edit, read the source authority and the target section. If the pointer would contradict existing target semantics, require line-cite updates, retire or introduce FORWARD-REF/ACTIVE status flips, change local mechanics, or modify more than pointer placement, mechanical-mirror does NOT apply; fire fresh T1.

### Empirical evidence trail (n=2 self-observed + n=1 user-triggered same-session 2026-05-02 — codification basis)

| # | Ship | Mirror source | Mirror target | LOC | Trigger |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | #239 | sota-researcher.md initialPrompt (Ship #229 commit `30aee32`) | sota-research SKILL.md (commit `a695a76`) | +21/-3 | self-observed |
| 2 | #240 | Ship #239 sota-research SKILL.md (commit `a695a76`) | research-protocol.md (commit `7d9cb65`) | +13/-0 | self-observed |
| 3 | #241 | feedback_never_paper_as_primary_source_only_repos_2026_05_02.md (n=1 user-trigger) | CLAUDE.md L99-110 9-cohort menu (commit `dce4625`) | +7/-5 | user-triggered |

The three-ship trail establishes same-session recurrence for this exception. Per `.claude/rules/codification-threshold.md` (trigger-type jurisdiction + bounded-ship sizing) and `.claude/rules/named-failure-modes.md` §Promotion threshold (n=3 UNCODIFIED → dedicated-rule convention), the n=2-self-observed + n=1-user-triggered ladder satisfies the promotion gate. Verification query: `git log --grep="mechanical-mirror" --since="2026-05-02" --oneline` returns the 3 commits above.

### Anti-patterns

- **Predicate violated** (new mechanics inline / new authority-cite-status class introduced / unsettled source authority / target-surface contradiction) → mechanical-mirror does NOT apply; fire fresh T1 per Pattern A
- **Upstream verdict unresolved or negative** — source T1 verdict is REJECT, INCONCLUSIVE, timeout-with-red-flags, or NEEDS-REVISION with unapplied prescriptions; mechanical-mirror does NOT apply; STOP or fire fresh T1 per `.claude/rules/cross-model-consensus.md` §Verdict shapes
- **Skip T2 or rely on degraded T2** → violates `.claude/rules/cross-model-consensus.md` §The contract HARD GATE; T2 commit-time hook is REQUIRED as the cross-model verification net. If `codex_t2_pre_commit_gate.py` is disabled, STRICT/FAIL_CLOSED is off, the commit path bypasses the hook, or T2 returns no usable verdict, mechanical-mirror does NOT apply; fire fresh T1 or block commit.
- **Retroactive application** — mechanical-mirror is forward-only per `.claude/rules/port-note-discipline.md` §6 anti-pattern "Do not rewrite historical commit bodies"
- **Recursive self-application during codification** — the codification ship for THIS exception (Ship #242) CANNOT invoke its own exception because the rule does not exist pre-commit. Codification ships introduce net-new-mechanics; they require fresh T1 per Pattern A regardless of how mechanical the propagation appears.
- **Looks-mechanical rationalization** — "small docs edit", "mechanical", or "mirror-looking" alone is insufficient; all activation predicates must be checked from direct file reads before commit. This preserves `.claude/rules/cross-model-consensus.md` §Anti-patterns, which explicitly refutes generic T1 skips because an edit "looks mechanical".
- **Evidence-count inflation / re-promotion churn** — after n=3 codifies the owner rule, later same-class mirrors may update evidence only when materially useful; they do not re-promote or widen the exception unless a new failure mode emerges.
